Glasgow archive/Sources/Census
Catalogue · 27 Sep 2026

The source library

Census

Households and population returns, arranged by census year and jurisdiction. Coverage and the meaning of a household relationship vary by census.

2,668 collected reference items · 1,415 linked catalogue people

By country & location

Canada 62 referencesEngland 104 referencesIreland 212 referencesScotland 162 referencesUnited States 1,869 references

Use the collection search for a county, parish, townland or address. Newspaper country groups identify the place of publication; the subject may have lived elsewhere.

People in the collected census references

2,668 reference items · 1,415 linked catalogue people. Several citations may describe the same household; one return can name several people. Linked people include spouses and relatives and are not a verified count of Glasgow surname entries. This is our collected evidence, not a complete surname search of the surviving census.

2,531 references have a supported census year; 137 remain unresolved. Years come from the enumeration title or original census URL; access dates, birth years and archive numbers are excluded. Countries identify the census jurisdiction where stated, rather than a person’s birthplace.

Collected references 2,668

Reference items in this project, not the total surviving archive. A reference can appear in several types or locations; several references can concern the same record. Countries are inferred from source descriptions and recorded places, so check the citation. Undated and ambiguous items remain available.

  • "Littleton" Glasgow was born on 3 March about 1872 -1873 in Pennsylvania . He was the child of Lydia Ballard and Civil War veteran Abraham Glasgow of the famous 54th Massachusetts Volunteer Infantry. He was named after his maternal grandfather, Littleton "Little" Ballard.
